### Task 5.4A pre-implementation contract review (2026-10-20)

Owner requested Task 5.4A only. Reviewed latest 5.3C handoff, Doc 05 governing policy, BTR-08, Doc 04 API-090/093 and reserved API-336/337/338, generation/sealing source, pair QA, locked renderer, private PDF grants/storage and worker fencing. **Contract review only: Task 5.4A remains TODO; all task counts/statuses and prior gates unchanged.** No implementation/verification claim.

**Confirmed reusable boundaries:** `inventory.py` generates the original `safereturn-production-insert-v1` with pair references, Lost/ICE tags and concealed activation secrets, encrypts once into `safereturn-sealed-insert-v1`, stores canonical sealed JSON in the generation idempotency receipt, and stores its SHA-256 on the batch. Public-key fingerprint currently hashes the configured PEM bytes (not a DER-normalized key). The receipt request hash incorporates that fingerprint but the receipt does not separately expose it as batch-bound metadata. Export cannot decrypt the insert to inspect its count/identities. Preserve the original bytes/checksum and API-090 behavior; missing/ambiguous provenance must conflict, not reconstruct/reseal. DB current-child/count checks cannot alone certify the contents of an opaque legacy ciphertext.

Existing `require_staff` supplies verified-account/live role/current MFA enforcement, with session validation upstream; recent signed AMR remains an additional export check. Existing print jobs, grants and provider adapter are order/PDF-specific: grants bind actor rather than the required batch-export session; `PrintStore` enforces PDF MIME/magic, private-order-print bucket and order-print keys. Do not reuse that bucket or grant schema unchanged for sealed inserts. Worker claim/attempt/clock fencing and encrypted receipt primitives are reusable patterns, not authorization for batch production. Locked renderer remains credential-free; API-094 physical pair QA is separate from encrypted handoff and Task 6.4 release.

**Approval required before affected code/schema; these are proposals, not approved contracts:**

1. **Printer and independent custody authority:** approve an additive immutable batch-scoped authority record naming the authorized operations executor, distinct security approver, exact generation recipient PEM fingerprint, sealed checksum, approval reference, current revision and revocation state. Specify who may provision/revoke it through controlled offline operations, how executor/approver independence is enforced for multi-role accounts, and which changes invalidate approval. No assumed superadmin inheritance, self-approval, role editor, new permission or public authority endpoint. BTR-08 requires this authority but does not define its provisioning or storage contract.
2. **Frozen source and durable persistence:** approve additive schema after pending 0026 for batch export jobs/events, original sealed-payload provenance, encrypted idempotent receipts and session-bound grants with least-privilege RLS/helpers. Specify how an existing API-090 receipt is unambiguously linked to a batch and original recipient without guessing from current configuration or a one-way request hash; ambiguous/missing source conflicts. Freeze the original canonical sealed bytes/hash, pair/child metadata and generation provenance; never rewrite generation history. Approve revision ownership, job states, allowed reason codes and bounded bytes/attempts/lease limits. No retention period or credential recovery policy is proposed. Any migration must be drafted and rehearsed offline before separate application approval.
3. **Exact transport DTOs and consumption:** BTR-08 defines API-093 request/202 fields and API-338 request, but API-336 metadata response, API-338 response/token-header encoding, error/status matrix, revision increments, idempotency scope/expiry and replay after grant supersession/consumption are unspecified. Approve closed DTOs, same-origin mutation checks, authorization before replay, safe metadata only, and session-bound one-use encrypted-byte consumption (BTR-08 maximum 300 seconds, 256-bit digest-stored verifier, duplicate consumption 410). Decide whether the original canonical sealed JSON is served directly from encrypted durable storage or copied to a dedicated approved private object class; if object-backed, approve exact MIME/bucket/key/size/provider access semantics. Existing PDF storage configuration grants no authority for this new object class. No public URL, plaintext browser manifest or ordinary admin credential projection.
4. **Acknowledgment, reconciliation and re-export:** approve the exact signed/checksummed handoff record format, verification trust root, independent recorder/approver authority and controlled ingestion procedure. Define what distinguishes a safe same-byte retry from unknown printer acknowledgment, consumed download, accepted handoff and already released batch; identify the source of release state. BTR-08 requires conflicts for unknown acknowledgment/already released/stale authority and forbids a second physical run, but supplies no authoritative record schema or writer contract. No acknowledgment endpoint, automatic acceptance, physical-release writer, destruction policy or recovery policy is inferred.

**Prepared verification scope after approval:** synthetic/in-memory guarded API and provider stand-ins only: anonymous/expired sessions; wrong role/printer/custodian, revoked authority, MFA/recent AMR; version/idempotency/re-export/grant replay and competing consumers; unchanged opaque payload/hash and immutable history; exactly one current Lost + ICE and count mismatch; secret canaries/safe metadata/errors; bounded competing workers/stale leases; partial/lost-ack upload, checksum mismatch, rollback and retry. Scoped Ruff/format/isolated compile (no configured backend typechecker); frontend checks only if frontend changes become necessary. Preserve isolated output under `output/task54a` and any frontend build under `tmp/task54a-web`, with no live-server promotion. Database RLS/ACL/locks/migrations/rollback/worker fencing, actual private provider and physical/native-art/custody acceptance remain separate required gates, not simulated proof.

**Evidence/preservation:** read-only source review; no tests, build, SQL, provider call or migration rehearsal performed because no implementation/schema has been approved for the missing contracts. Only this handoff addition changed in this review. Branch `codex/implemented-workflows-through-57i`, existing dirty files, API-339 and Tasks 5.2A/5.3A/5.3B/5.3C preserved. Installed 0021 and unapplied 0022–0026 retained by no DB access; owner operations AND finance and configured authentication retained by noninterference. ### Task 5.7B pre-implementation dependency/contract review (2026-10-20)

Owner requested Task 5.7B only. Reviewed latest 5.4A handoff, Doc 05 governing policy, frontend/AGENTS.md, dashboard metric register/permission matrix and ADM-01, current /admin shell/capability projection, canonical inventory, finance reconciliation, fulfillment, notification, support and storage/catalog guards. **Review only: Task 5.7B remains TODO; all statuses/counts and prior gates unchanged.** No application code, endpoint or migration added.

**Dependency findings:** 5.7A is IMPLEMENTED (live role/MFA shell exists; module accessibility/native acceptance remains). 2.3C is TODO: no approved registered-user helper, DTO or role grant; do not query general Auth tables or approximate users from profiles/sessions. 3.5A is IMPLEMENTED: API-230 canonical inventory counts exist, but low-stock threshold/operational interpretation still needs approval. 5.3C is IMPLEMENTED: API-342/343 stored-state counts exist, feature disabled and 0026 unapplied; actual PostgreSQL/finance interpretation remains gated. 5.5A is IMPLEMENTED: API-080/246/247 metadata reads exist with remaining contract/manual gates retained. None becomes VERIFIED through this task. Query foundations for 5.7C/D/E/F remain separate; no directory/lifecycle/finance/print-console implementation is authorized here.

**Missing contracts:** Doc 04 has no dashboard aggregate/trend route or closed DTO; API-344 onwards are unallocated. The metric register requires approval of metric-role visibility, exact predicates/time windows and thresholds before charts. Existing /admin shows permitted module links only. Existing staff capabilities describe detail operations, not a blanket aggregate grant. Registered-user breakdowns, settlement/revenue deduplication/time basis, activation event history and generic worker-health thresholds are not sufficiently specified. Request approval of A–D below before affected code/schema; proposals are not approvals.

**A — Explicit dependency treatment and scope.** Implement the bounded operational ADM-01 dashboard only. Do not implement 2.3C or another task; omit registered-user counts entirely until its separately approved helper/role contract exists. Keep gross/net/profit/refund-adjusted money metrics, bank comparisons, scan/location analytics, activation-history series and inferred provider/backup health unavailable pending their own approved evidence/contracts. Show known dependency/configuration gaps without fabricated numbers; do not relabel an incomplete task complete. Preserve existing module links. English/Urdu, RTL, accessible chart tables, independent widget failures and freshness are in scope. A broader dashboard remains gated by these dependencies; implementation may be IMPLEMENTED, not VERIFIED.

**B — Transport, DTO and permissions.** Propose API-344 GET `/admin/dashboard/summary` and API-345 GET `/admin/dashboard/trends`; allocate only after approval. Both reuse genuine verified account/live session/current signed+provider MFA and explicit role checks; no superadmin inheritance. No mutating endpoint or new role/capability grant. Feature disabled by default; read-only same-origin server relay, fixed target and strict safe response parsing. Closed summary `{as_of,groups}`; each authorized group `{group,status:ready|unavailable,as_of,metrics,reason_code}`; each metric `{metric_id,unit,count:{value:0..1000,is_exact:boolean},drilldown}`; unavailable groups have no metrics/as_of and a fixed safe reason. At cap 1000, is_exact=false (display 1000+). Unauthorized groups are absent, including unavailable/reason/series metadata. Count units are explicitly product|variant|pair|qr_identity|order|attempt|job|shipment|outbox_event|case|sync_record. No rows, customer/user/QR IDs, tokens, addresses, money, bank/evidence/provider/storage references or free text. Trends `{as_of,range_start,range_end,bucket_timezone:UTC,display_timezone,series}`; each authorized series `{series_id,unit,status,reason_code,buckets:[{start,end,count}]}`, failed series has empty buckets. Only display_timezone UTC|Asia/Karachi; no arbitrary range/filter/group/SQL input, repeated/unknown parameters denied. Anonymous/expired session 401, nonstaff/no permitted dashboard group or MFA denial 403, validation 422, feature/outer dependency failure 503; independently failed authorized groups remain unavailable within 200. Private/no-store/nosniff and safe correlation/errors on every response; no browser storage/analytics.

Group-role allowlists are proposed extensions for these aggregate reads, mirroring current detail guards exactly: catalog=operations/security_admin/superadmin; inventory/printing/fulfillment=operations; finance=finance; notifications=operations/security_admin; support=support with the exact API-097 assigned-to-self OR unassigned scope; finance_mirror=finance/security_admin/superadmin. Multi-role accounts get the union of independently authorized groups; owner retains operations AND finance. No global support/privacy, blanket audit, general worker inventory or new user-directory access. Feature-disabled finance reconciliation data is unavailable, never bypassed by calling its SQL helper directly.

**C — Exact counting semantics and warnings.** Current-state summary uses independent distinct-entity aggregates, no item/tag/attempt fanout. Catalog products by draft/published/archived and variants by active flag separately. Inventory reuses API-230 available/reserved/allocated predicates and current QR stored statuses; active never implies public/medical publication. Unset existing low-stock threshold remains unconfigured; no new threshold is chosen. Finance review queue counts distinct payment_attempts in submitted/under_review, independent of order count; reconciliation preserves exact API-343 seven reasons/affected-order cap and unavailable bank-history checks. Orders are distinct orders by the existing independent order/payment/fulfillment enums. Ready-to-ship count copies the complete canonical API-232 predicate, including current eligibility and existing shipment exclusion; not payment-only. Shipments by canonical preparing/packed/dispatched/delivered/exception/returned, with returned distinct from exception; eligibility-lost preparing/packed/dispatched packages are a separate requires_review signal. Printing counts distinct existing print_jobs by actual stored enums (failed is not a durable dead job); awaiting physical QA means ready/downloaded job with retained current artifact and no successful artifact-matching QA under existing canonical checks. Do not count a PDF download as QA. Notification outbox events by pending/leased/retry/sent/dead; expired lease is an operational state observation, not a new provider failure or retry attempt. Support open/investigating counts use the same per-actor case scope as list reads, not global privacy/erasure completion. Finance mirror sync records by pending/synced/retry/failed, never settlement truth. Reconciliation/failed/dead/exception/eligibility-loss counts greater than zero produce fixed needs_review warnings with authorized owner/runbook/drill-down; no aging/SLA/duration threshold, automatic remediation or healthy-provider inference. Drill-downs must resolve to existing implemented authorized pages with supported filters; counts lacking a usable reviewed drill-down remain unavailable rather than inventing a later 5.7C/D/E/F screen. Final SQL predicates and exact route/filter mapping must be recorded in Doc 04 before implementation.

**D — Trends, query bounds and evidence.** Propose 30 completed UTC calendar-day buckets `[00:00 UTC today minus 30 days,00:00 UTC today)`; display timezone changes labels only, never bucket membership. Series: placed orders by non-null orders.placed_at (operations or finance), dispatched shipments by non-null shipments.dispatched_at (operations), created notification outbox events by created_at (operations/security_admin). Each source entity is counted once per its own timestamp; current cancellations/returns do not erase placement/dispatch observations. These are currently retained rows observed now, not a reconstructed historical snapshot or lifetime/public-scan series. No settlement/revenue or activation trend until separately defined evidence exists. Daily counts preserve 1000+ cap semantics. Server-side bounded aggregate helpers only, 3-second statement timeout per independently isolated group with a bounded overall request budget and finite group/series list; preserve partial failures with savepoints/separate bounded transactions and explicit per-group as_of, no false globally atomic-snapshot claim. Any necessary additive migration follows pending 0027, read helpers only; inspect/emit/rehearse offline, no application or installed-schema claim. Actual EXPLAIN/index/cardinality/RLS/revocation/MVCC/resource bounds remain required PostgreSQL gates. The following A–E are **proposals, not approvals**. Owner approval would authorize only this read-only Task2.3C implementation; it would not authorize migrations being applied, runtime activation or 2.3D/5.7C.

**A — Roles and transport.** Propose API-346 GET `/admin/users`, API-347 GET `/admin/users/{user_id}`, API-348 GET `/admin/users/count`. All three explicitly allow `security_admin` only; superadmin alone, support, operations and finance are denied. **B — Closed DTOs and account-state semantics.** List `{as_of,items,next_cursor}`; each item `{user_id,registered_at,email_verified,provider_access}`. Detail `{as_of,user:{user_id,registered_at,email_verified,provider_access},application_suspension:{status:"unavailable",reason_code:"NOT_IMPLEMENTED"}}`. `user_id` UUID; registered_at aware UTC Auth created_at; email_verified = email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL; provider_access = `banned` iff banned_until > observation time, otherwise `not_banned`. This is provider restriction metadata, never a claim of usable sessions/MFA or application-active status. Do not expose ban expiry/reasons or claim future application suspension is active/false. Same registered population for list/detail/count: retained Auth rows with deleted_at IS NULL and NOT COALESCE(is_anonymous,false), including unverified and provider-banned registrations, with or without a profile/session. Deleted/anonymous/missing detail all return concealed404. No display name, email (including masked), phone, contact, medical/location/profile content, role list, credentials, identities, factors, sessions, last login, private case content or arbitrary metadata. No field expansion.

**C — Search and pagination.** Permit exact `user_id` UUID search only; no name/email/phone/prefix/fuzzy/full-text search. Optional `email_verified=true|false` and `provider_access=banned|not_banned` filters on list/count; count accepts the same exact user_id predicate. List limit1..100(default20), fixed registered_at DESC/user_id DESC order, limit+1 keyset paging with strict bounded opaque versioned cursor bound to filters. No offset/custom sort/page totals/client-side enumeration. Reject unknown/repeated parameters and malformed/oversized/filter-mismatched cursors422; no SQL wildcard interpretation. Across requests this is current-state paging, not a frozen snapshot: concurrent registration/deletion/state changes may alter membership; no false stable-total promise. No total count on list. UUID is a controlled staff identifier, not an invitation to enumerate publicly.

**D — Registered-user counts and support authority.** Count `{as_of,definition:"retained_nonanonymous_auth_registrations_v1",filters:{user_id,email_verified,provider_access},count:{value,is_exact}}`; absent filters explicitly null. **Missing/conflicting contracts:**(1) No account action subject-binding/authority table, approval lifetime/revision/reason binding, or independent executor policy. Support opener/order owner/pair owner is not approved action authority. (2) Exact reason enums, closed result/recovery DTO, initial revision and duplicate-state semantics absent. (3) No named recoverable-custodian evidence model or serializable last-custodian protocol. (4)0005 inspects only sessions.id/user_id/not_after/aal, not immutable session creation metadata. AMR reauthentication on an old session cannot prove a new session. No actual managed session metadata read is authorized by this in-memory-only task; created_at availability/immutability must remain an actual-provider/PG activation gate. **A — Command transport, closed schemas, roles and results.** Implement only reserved API320/321/322. All require explicit security_admin, verified account/live subject-bound session/signed+provider AAL2 plus existing recent signed AMR within300s (never token iat), strict single configured same-origin header, no query parameters and actor/operation/subject-scoped Idempotency-Key. Default-disabled account_lifecycle_enabled and unset account_lifecycle_web_origin; fail503 for disabled/missing dependencies without fallback. All bodies exactly `{case_id:UUID,expected_revision:integer1..2147483646,reason_code,confirmed:true}`; no explanations/evidence/secrets supplied to API. Reasons: suspension=suspected_compromise|abuse_review|security_review; restoration=review_cleared; recovery=factor_loss|account_access_review. Implicit unseen account state is active/revision1/epochNULL. Successful suspend/restore200 `{user_id,case_id,action:suspend|restore,state:active|suspended,revision,event_id,occurred_at,fresh_sign_in_required:true}`; successful recovery202 `{user_id,case_id,action:recovery_escalated,revision,event_id,occurred_at,status:referred}`. Recovery appends a referral and advances revision but changes neither state nor invalidation epoch and performs no delivery/provider action. All timestamps UTC. No full contact/profile/case/role/credential/session metadata. No capability or support/superadmin grant.

**B — Exact case subject and independent action authority.** New controlled-provisioned account_case_bindings selects one explicit user UUID for one case; never infer from opener/order/pair. Case must be open/investigating and binding current. One unexpired/unrevoked single-use account_action_approval binds case/subject/executor/action/reason/expected_revision, approval UUID and a distinct security_admin approver (different from executor and target); actor cannot target itself for any of the three actions. Approval is provisioned outside the web app through an authorized privileged channel; no new approval/grant writer endpoint. Approver's current role/account and custody evidence rechecked at execution. Account actions neither read/decrypt case description/notes nor claim/assign/change support cases. Missing/foreign binding/approval/subject all concealed404 after auth. New action approvals must be reissued after a state/revision change; receipt replay rechecks live actor/role/recent-AMR/case/binding/independent approver/revocation and the exact original consumed approval, without demanding its old expected_revision match the now-advanced target state. Receipt cannot resurrect a revoked approval or authorize a different command.

**C — Custodian evidence and lockout.** Controlled-provisioned security_custodians registry records user UUID, independent verifier UUID, opaque evidence UUID, expiry and revocation; no recovery secret/identity documents in app. Distinct authorized verifier required; no self-attestation or seed of the owner. A qualifying remaining custodian needs unrevoked registry evidence, active explicit security_admin role, live retained/nonanonymous/email-confirmed/non-provider-banned account and nonsuspended application state. Before suspension preserve at least one qualifying custodian other than target; missing/expired registry evidence fails closed. A global lifecycle/custodian transaction lock serializes lifecycle checks; future controlled role/custody provisioning must honor the same lock protocol, with raw privileged/provider-side races explicitly outside the in-memory proof. Do not claim this task implements2.3B provisioning or establishes an actual named custodian. Real custody/independent-approval drill remains a gate.

**D — Fresh sessions and global request serialization.** Pending0030 after0029 adds application state/security events/bindings/approvals/custody tables and narrow helpers, preserving prior migrations on disk. Reconcile managed auth.sessions.created_at timestamptz metadata in migration; missing/mismatched metadata fails closed. An additive0030 replacement of0005's helper retains its signature/returned booleans and existing provider predicates, adds actor application suspension and strict `session.created_at > invalidate_before` admission, and obtains an actor-scoped shared transaction lock before observing state. No general runtime Auth SELECT, no managed Auth mutation/index. Null state uses active revision1. Suspend AND restore set epoch to database clock time after exclusive target lock; an old session cannot regain admission by refreshed token/fresh AMR. Target-exclusive lock acquisition must be nonblocking/try-lock, returning409 contention/retry, since requests already hold actor shared locks and two cross-target security actions could otherwise deadlock. All protected requests using the existing session helper retain the actor shared lock until transaction completion; lifecycle target-exclusive lock therefore fences in-flight protected mutations. Recheck executing actor role/session/MFA/recent-AMR and approval inside the command transaction. Do not use a flag to bypass enforcement for persisted suspended/invalidated accounts: feature flag governs new commands; state guard remains authoritative after eventual installation. Actual provider creation-time immutability/new-session semantics, SQL volatility/lock behavior and MVCC are acceptance gates, not inferred from synthetic timestamps. No live installation/config/Auth change in this task.

**E — Durable denial, idempotency, grant/job boundaries and rollback.** No same-state success: new suspend-on-suspended or restore-on-active409; stale version412; missing key400; wrong role/AAL/AMR/Origin403; expired/revoked session401; invalid closed input422; feature/config/crypto/DB failure503. Same key+canonical body replays encrypted receipt only after current authority checks; changed body409. Persist state/event/minimal allowlisted audit/approval consumption/receipt atomically; immutable events and original history retained. Narrow application eligibility/epoch helper must permanently fence subject-dependent grants and uncompleted jobs/approvals whose relevant subject/executor/approver authority predates the invalidation epoch; restoration never reauthorizes old authority. Exact inventory of privacy-export request/approval/download grants, manufacturing executor/approver/job/download grants, and staff print-download grants must be recorded in Doc04/03 with existing timestamps/relationships and tested before code changes to those admission points; no change to their original migration files, completed/accepted history, ownership/payment/stock/printed public QR or business delivery/outbox jobs that do not depend on continuing subject authority. If another affected grant/job lacks a safe epoch binding, deny affected admission and document unavailable; do not choose a new recovery/reapproval policy silently. No worker/provider execution in testing. For0030 rollback, offline forward-disable/deny commands only once state/events exist; never provide a downgrade that drops durable security state or restores admission to old sessions. Empty-schema-only offline rollback may remove new objects and restore the prior helper after proving no state/events; real rollback/forward correction requires separate authorization.

**A — Pages and explicit navigation authority.** Implement /admin/users and /admin/users/[userId] with existing AdminPage/AdminShell/provider-verified live session/MFA and explicit security_admin checks. Standalone superadmin/support/operations/finance denied; multi-role union only through explicit security_admin. Add only fixed /admin/users to the MFA return allowlist; dynamic subject/query/external return paths remain rejected. MFA step-up returns to the list, then a fresh detail read; no subject data carried in an untrusted return URL. No new dashboard metric or role/custody/approval editor.

**B — Explicit opt-in API347 read extension, preserving defaults.** Approve a single optional `include=account_security` query on existing API347 only. No query preserves exactly the existing top-level/user DTO and application_suspension unavailable/NOT_IMPLEMENTED. Repeated/unknown/other include values422. Opt-in response retains existing safe user metadata and uses closed application_suspension union: ready `{status:"ready",state:"active"|"suspended",revision:integer1..2147483647,as_of:UTC,commands_available:boolean}` or unavailable `{status:"unavailable",reason_code:"STATE_UNAVAILABLE"}`. This is authoritative application state, separate from provider_access; absent state row means active/revision1 only after the reviewed state helper/schema is available. commands_available means command feature/origin configuration permits attempting a request; it is **not** case approval, recent-AMR proof, custody proof or action eligibility. No invalidation epoch/session IDs/role/contact/case/approval/custody details or reason text. Missing state/helper/schema/failed state subquery yields the opt-in unavailable marker (never inferred active); authentication/role/session denial clears the entire projection rather than being masked as unavailability. Retain final live assurance recheck and private/no-store/nosniff. List/count DTOs/predicates/search/filters/caps unchanged.

**C — Narrow additive helper/storage boundary.** Pending0031 after0030 would add one API-only pinned read helper for opt-in application state/revision, using explicit security_admin and existing verified live signed+provider MFA context, retained/nonanonymous target scope and existing0030 state table. No general Auth SELECT/table grant, writer, new security state copy or prior migration edits. Read via bounded3s savepoint; helper/schema failure rolls back the subquery and reports STATE_UNAVAILABLE, with current assurance failures still denied. Observation is current state, not a frozen snapshot with provider directory metadata; no globally atomic claim. No new backend API IDs:320–322/339/344/345/346–348 remain allocated unchanged, future IDs349+. Additive helper-only offline downgrade drops only0031 helper, never0030 state/guards/events. **D — Fixed server adapters and safe interactions.** Approve same-origin Next route /admin/users/api, separate from page.tsx. Redirects denied, no-store, finite10s deadline, max64KiB JSON response/max2KiB command body, strict closed response parsing, no raw upstream messages/stack/details, fixed safe allowlisted error codes only (including REAUTH_REQUIRED/STALE_VERSION/CONTENTION/IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED/INVALID_STATE/LAST_CUSTODIAN) for bilingual handling.
